    | Renderer branch timing and absolute alignment hardening (2026-08-15):
    | Every live conditional branch in `libraries/vcs/renderers/` now carries an
    | explicit `.same` or `.cross` page contract; `vcs_renderer_branch_contracts.pl`
    | rejects bare or `.flex` renderer branches.  The all-five kernels were adjusted
    | where a cycle-balanced path required an unconditional jump rather than relying
    | on an accidentally same-page conditional branch.  Relocatable o26 `.align N`
    | now means final absolute alignment: the assembler records a component alignment
    | and selected base phase, and the linker places the component at that residue so
    | the aligned interior address remains absolute after relocation.  o26 alignment
    | boundaries are presently restricted to powers of two; direct/HEX assembly keeps
    | the older arbitrary-positive-boundary behavior.  The PAL50 and SECAM50 all-five
    | examples use the stock 192-line all-five renderer and now remain phase-stable;
    | the Stella regression checks PF write phases in addition to frame length.
    | Validation baseline is 499/499 compile tests and 305/305 E2E tests.
    | 
    | Disassembler side project (2026-08-16):
    | `.../disassembler.txt` is the focused context/roadmap. Hybrid priorities H0-H2
    | are complete. H0 reuses the maintained MOS6502 core for trusted-topology RESET discovery
    | with exact PC/A/X/Y/SP/P, RIOT RAM, mapper state, and ROM provenance. H1 adds abstract
    | SP/stack-page aliasing, conservative provable BRK/IRQ/RTI continuations, exhaustive first-
    | pass SWCHB startup-state coverage (all 8 COLOR/BW+difficulty combinations; seven redundant
    | runs are equivalence-elided only when the all-high execution never reads SWCHB), SELECT/RESET
    | press-release scenarios when SWCHB is read, demand-driven SWCHA/INPT scenarios, and concrete bus
    | models for every supported non-coprocessor mapper. H2 feeds only fully exact static
    | CPU+128-byte RIOT states back into concrete execution, unions new targets into the CFG,
    | and repeats to a bounded fixed point; mapper-owned RAM/transient state that cannot be
    | supplied exactly blocks static-to-concrete seeding rather than being guessed. On
    | c33caa7d6ac7251bb804e0473198901d.bin, 34 startup/input scenarios discover 299 ROM
    | instruction starts and 57 executed RIOT-RAM instruction starts; the SWCHA alternate path
    | reaches the loader, copies the 128-byte game into RIOT RAM, and executes it from $00C2
    | with ROM provenance.
    | The new
    | `disassembler/vcsc-disas` now has an exact-round-trip first implementation with
    | all-opcode decode/emission, mandatory branch `.same`/`.cross`, unbanked and
    | F8/F6/F4(+Superchip), FA, DPC and WD/WDSW bank-aware analysis, per-bank origin inference, overlapping
    | instruction streams, established/speculative confidence, code-as-data and
    | unclassified-byte roles, TIA/RIOT symbols
    | with exact mirror comments, conservative graphics rows, static plus bounded dynamic
    | video inference, controller inference, and explicit analysis overrides. A run with zero established
    | instructions is a hard error; raw `.byte` fallback is used only for uncertain regions inside an
    | otherwise successful disassembly. Video inference now
    | accumulates arbitrary statically-known TIM64T values, parameterized timer-helper
    | arguments recovered at JSR call sites, broad counted-WSYNC loop lengths, and
    | bounded filename region-token hints rather than relying mainly on four scheduler
    | constants. When established code writes VSYNC, vcsc-disas also runs a bounded
    | dynamic frame probe using the shared simulator/mos6502 CPU core with 6507 address
    | mirroring, RIOT RAM/timers, TIA VSYNC/WSYNC stalls, supported bank hotspots, and
    | neutral controller inputs. Several consecutive frames must stabilize before the
    | measurement is accepted. Measurements are reported as raw 76-cycle line intervals;
    | the maintained schedulers deliberately measure 264/314 raw intervals while Stella
    | reports 262/312 displayed scanlines. DPC remains static-only until the probe has a
    | faithful DPC coprocessor/register model. Generated assembly
    | omits redundant `.z`/`.zx`/`.zy` suffixes for ordinary named instructions while
    | retaining `.a`/`.ax`/`.ay` whenever a low absolute operand would otherwise relax;
    | ambiguous raw `opXX` forms keep their required explicit suffixes. References into
    | interior bytes of emitted instructions or vector `.word` containers use the
    | container label plus an exact offset, so targets such as an IRQ vector value of
    | `$FFFF` never create an unplaceable `L_FFFF` label. If vector-table bytes are
    | themselves established executable code, the vector `.word` yields to instruction
    | emission so entries inside the vector table remain labelable; Stellar Track's
    | NMI bytes at `$FFFA/$FFFB` exercise this case. The standalone
    | `disassembler/roundtrip.pl INPUT_DIR OUTPUT_DIR` drives vcsc-disas and vcsc-as,
    | retains .s26/rebuilt .bin files, prints MD5 values, and requires exact byte
    | identity. Focused disassembler Items 0-22 and 29 are complete; Items 23-28 queue
    | GL, CM, DPC+, CDF/CDFJ/CDFJ+, the remaining NMI-vector cleanup, and
    | playable malformed/odd-size dumps. Item 28 is now complete for the two Stella-playable
    | 4096+/-2 corpus cases: 4098-byte dumps use the first 4096 bytes as the logical 4K cart and
    | preserve the two-byte tail raw; 4094-byte dumps zero-fill two logical bytes for analysis but
    | emit only the physical 4094 bytes. Both real hashes round-trip exactly and match Stella as 4K.
    | Starpath/Supercharger AR fast-load support now
    | recognizes exact 8448-byte multiples, reconstructs header-mapped 6K RAM payloads,
    | and preserves physical load bytes exactly. E7 now models the 8K/12K/16K
    | selector tables, fixed final ROM, both split-RAM regions, and exact CFG state.
    | Main roadmap Items 41 and 42 are complete. Bankswitching Items 31-37 are complete,
    | Item 38 remains deferred, and Items 39-44 queue public diagnostics/profile work
    | for E0, 3F, 3E, FE, WD, and DPC. Main-roadmap Item 43 is next. The current
    | compile suite passes 500/500. Four vcsc-disas-focused E2E regressions pass, with
    | 118 semantic fixtures and all 256 opcode bytes exact. The hardening fuzz corpus
    | uses two deterministic images for every supported size/mapper family (30 accepted
    | images total), and all 88 editable
    | VCSC example ROMs are byte-exact disassemble/reassemble gates inside the normal
    | eight example-build shards. Native staged vcsc-disas -> staged vcsc-as passes,
    | and the static Linux package builds as a seven-tool bundle containing vcsc-disas;
    | Windows package wiring is source-hygiene pinned on this non-MinGW host. The E2E
    | inventory is now 318 cases. One unrelated legacy renderer case can exceed its
    | explicit 12-second harness limit in this container but passes when run directly.
    | Commercial ARMAMBSH, Bridge, Stellar Track, the standard 10495-byte Pitfall II
    | DPC ROM, Mountain King's 12288-byte CBS FA/RAM Plus ROM, and both known Pursuit of the Pink Panther WD/WDSW layouts all round-trip exactly. DPC is inferred
    | from its distinctive size/layout and its first
    | 8K is analyzed as two F8-style program banks while the trailing DPC data/RNG bytes
    | remain explicit auxiliary data. FA is inferred from the 12K layout as three 4K
    | banks with fixed startup bank 2 and `$1FF8-$1FFA` selectors; its `$1000-$11FF`
    | RAM overlay hides the first `$200` bytes of each bank from runtime ROM analysis.
    | Mountain King exercises mirrored origins `$3000/$5000/$7000` and round-trips
    | exactly. Stella 7.0 identifies Bridge and Stellar Track
    | as NTSC, and the broadened static detector now reports Bridge as NTSC/medium and
    | Stellar Track as NTSC/high. Ambiguous size-compatible mappers require a
    | cartridge-backed RESET path; islands may extend but never establish one. Survivors
    | run to a fixed point. HLT/JAM/KIL normally eliminates a model; a narrow-selector
    | bank-changing edge can preserve a mapper against only an abstract possible halt,
    | but broad partial-address decoders get no such preference. Branch edges constrain
    | C/Z/N/V, preventing impossible complementary-branch JAM paths. Raw hotspot counts
    | are never ranked across mapper families; a switch that avoids old-mapping
    | HLT/JAM/KIL is strong evidence. This fixes accidental
    | mapper signatures spanning unrelated instructions (for example F8 `CPX #$2C` +
    | `BCS` producing Stella's raw UA `2C B0 0F` pattern) and lets both primary and
    | speculative execution continue in the newly selected bank even when the old-bank
    | next byte is JAM/KIL. The speculative island pass reverse-marks HLT/JAM/KIL reachability
    | on a mapper-aware physical-ROM graph, then runs the bounded stateful C/Z/N/V flow
    | walk only on those candidates. It promotes only nontrivial safe candidates immediately
    | after three-rejected-start barriers. Established code and overlaps remain authoritative. Real `JMP ($xxFF)`
    | instructions are emitted raw because vcsc-as intentionally rejects that hazardous
    | NMOS indirect-JMP spelling. Sprite/font/playfield detection tracks short straight-line A/X/Y provenance from
    | ROM loads through register transfers and simple ALU/shift transforms into
    | STA/STX/STY writes to GRP0/GRP1/PF0/PF1/PF2. It also recognizes dynamic graphics
    | pointers assembled from a ROM low-byte table plus a constant high byte; a constant
    | low-byte stride becomes frame-height evidence. Pitfall exercises this with eight
    | 22-row player frames at $FB00,$FB16,...,$FB9A. A supplemental high-confidence
    | structural pass recognizes long aligned runs of coherent 8x8 glyphs; Pitfall's
    | decimal font at $FF00 is the regression case. Raw `.byte` grouping now stops when
    | a graphics region begins so the first detected rows are not swallowed. A single
    | bitmap-shaped object and call-mediated/compressed-looking data still remain numeric
    | without stronger evidence. Parker Brothers E0 is now an explicit segmented 8K
    | mapper model with eight physical 1K banks, independent selectors for the first
    | three 1K runtime windows, fixed physical bank 7 at the top window, and reset
    | configuration 4/5/6/7. The initial seven-ROM Stella disagreement cluster
    | (Docastle, Ewok Adventure, Gyruss, Lord of the Rings, Popeye, Q-bert's Qubes,
    | and Super Cobra) now resolves E0 for all seven and round-trips each image
    | byte-exactly. A later nine-image Gyruss/Popeye cluster exposed a refinement bug:
    | its real E0 path stalls abstractly in an early RAM-clear loop, while a wrong E7
    | mapping can fabricate `$FFE4-$FFE6` selector traffic. E0 raw detector evidence now
    | retains Stella's E0-before-E7 precedence unless the E7 hypothesis has genuine E7
    | signature evidence; all nine now match Stella as E0. The assembler also computes
    | relative branches modulo the 16-bit CPU PC, so valid `$FFxx->$00xx` `.same/.cross`
    | branches no longer spuriously relax long. The semantic suite is 110/110 exact.
    | Synthetic RESET and speculative-island regressions pin the case
    | where an E0 selector is physically followed by JAM in the old bank but valid
    | execution continues in the selected bank. The semantic disassembler corpus is
    | now 105/105 exact. The standalone corpus verifier now has optional `--stella STELLA` differential mapper checking: it keeps SHA-256 in generated source, reuses its existing per-ROM MD5 for Stella compatibility, runs `STELLA -rominfo ROM`, normalizes the resolved Bankswitch Type (including Stella `2K* (1K)` as VCSC `1K`), and reports VCSC/Stella mapper MATCH/MISMATCH counts without treating Stella as authoritative; `--stella-strict` promotes mismatches to failure when explicitly desired. The normal hardening suite uses a fake Stella executable so this parser/normalization path is tested without an external emulator. WD/WDSW support models eight 1K banks selected as four-segment arrangements by TIA $30-$3F reads plus 64 bytes of cartridge RAM. Stella's names distinguish file layouts, not hardware: corrected 8192-byte images are WD when they carry the `A5 39 4C` (`LDA $39; JMP`) signature, while 8195-byte 8K+3 preservation dumps are WDSW. WDSW alone applies the historical bank-2/bank-3 file correction and preserves the three non-emulated trailing bytes exactly. The 2026-08-20 differential corpus now resolves `56210a3b9ea6d5dd8f417a357ed8ca92` as WDSW and `62ee2b8f59e9cd6285bbdb674a952e8b` as WD; requiring the Stella WD signature for automatic 8K WD hypotheses also removes false WD promotions of `850fdc358adda5dd8b7a8b9b178e6655` (UA) and `af1c6f6d73a00bc1be6b353fe9a27819` (F8). FC support now models Amiga Power Play's staged 4K-bank selector for 4K/8K/16K/32K images: `$1FF8` writes replace pending low bits, `$1FF9` writes add the representable high contribution or use Stella's modulo fallback, and any `$1FFC` access commits while code remains in the old bank before commit. RESET starts mapped/pending bank 0. Stella-compatible FC signatures identify all five FC cases in `problems.20260820_144844.zip`, and all five agree with Stella while the complete 32-ROM bundle remains byte-exact. Roadmap items 9, 10, 12, 13, and 16 are complete; Item 12 pointer-table presentation requires real low/high pointer-building data flow and never swallows interior labels, after ARMAMBSH exposed a shifted-byte false positive. Color-table presentation splits around interior labels instead of consuming them. Item 13 adds repeatable `--table` and even-length `--pointer` manual presentation hints on top of the existing mapper/origin/entry/code/data/video/controller overrides; manual data roles may overlap code without replacing executable source. Item 16 pins deterministic output, malformed/truncated failures, duplicated/padded preservation, all-supported-layout fuzzing, all 88 editable examples, install/package integration, and an independent stego smoke baseline. The expanded 30-image mapper fuzz test uses the long-test 120-second timeout because full disassemble/reassemble fuzzing is intentionally expensive under parallel test load. Items 17-22 add 3F/3E/FE/E7/WDSW/FC support; Items 23-26 remain; see `.../disassembler.txt`. Item 29 now adds Starpath/Arcadia AR structural support: each 8448-byte load is 8192 page-data bytes plus a 256-byte header, nonzero concatenated loads inherit reconstructed Supercharger RAM, and payload code is shown at runtime addresses with file provenance while physical bytes remain raw. All 85 AR files in the 2026-08-20 corpus round-trip exactly and match Stella; five bad page-checksum loads are preserved and annotated rather than repaired. Native 1024-byte cartridges are analyzed as unbanked 1K ROM mirrored through the 4K cartridge window. Item 27's IRQ/BRK half is now implemented: `$FFFE/$FFFF` is not a CFG root and is promoted only after a reachable BRK; legacy NMI-root behavior remains separately queued because the 6507 has no bonded NMI input either. Ms. Pac-Man PAL (`1ee9c1ba95cef2cf987d63f176c54ac3`) no longer fabricates the unreachable `$FFF9 BPL $003B` stream and round-trips exactly. The c33 1K loader remains the positive case where reachable BRK genuinely promotes the `$FC80` handler. Hybrid H0 concrete RESET discovery tracks exact CPU/SP state, RIOT RAM and ROM provenance, but now runs only with a trusted topology: unbanked 1K/2K/4K, explicit mapper override, or strong banked-mapper signature evidence. Weak size-default F8/F6/F4/FA guesses are suppressed after the OMNI example showed that executing an unsupported mapper as F4 could manufacture thousands of bogus instruction starts and invalid vector-label source. The dynamic video probe shares mapper IDs with the C disassembler through `dynamic_video_probe.h` (no duplicated numeric enum) and models 1K ROM mirroring explicitly. Exact 4K images with identical 2K halves are analyzed as 2K and preserve the duplicate raw.
    | 
    | 0840/EconoBanking uses physical file bank 0 as startup, with below-cartridge
    | selector families canonically represented by $0800/$0840. C26 selector metadata
    | now accepts the full 6507 physical $0000-$1FFF bus; below-window selectors do not
    | reserve cartridge ROM bytes. Generated selector bridges/trampolines use NMOS
    | absolute NOP-read opcode $0C for those addresses so registers/flags are preserved
    | without writing mirrored console devices. The simulator models that undocumented
    | NOP operand read and preserves underlying low-memory read/write behavior; public
    | `09_bankswitching/08_0840` and `vcs_0840.pl` certify read/write switching, reset
    | recovery, disassembly, and byte-exact round trip. Bankswitching Item 36 UA/UASW reuses this machinery with `(A & $1260)` aliases around $0220/$0240, paired profiles, reversed UASW association, public diagnostics, and exact round trips. Item 37 0FA0 is complete with `(A & $16E0)==$06A0/$06C0`, public `09_bankswitching/10_0fa0`, and exact disassembly. Item 38 remains deferred; Items 39-44 now backfill missing public diagnostics for E0/3F/3E/FE/WD/DPC.

    | The enhanced-asymmetric multisprite side quest is active in
    | `renderers/enhanced_multisprite_asymmetric/`; read `.../enhanced_asymmetric.txt`
    | before continuing it.  Dynamic non-reflected PF0/PF1/PF2 timing, per-event
    | colors, X=0..159 movement, top-band preload, Y=0..95 top reach, and the compact
    | unbanked 4K diagnostic are implemented.  The persistent-missing-sprite bugs are
    | fixed: close-above delta is no longer clobbered by `TAX`, and the directional
    | opposite-lane hazard is +1..+6.  Persistent fairness now records *all* omissions
    | per frame and stable-partitions omitted sprites ahead of shown sprites in overscan;
    | a two-independent-three-way-pile witness changed from service 5,5,2 / 5,5,2 to
    | 4,4,4 / 4,4,4, and the 250k Monte Carlo worst consecutive omission gap fell from
    | 4 to 2.  The real-CPU GRP oracle requires all six visibility counts to stay within
    | one frame over 97 checked frames.  Current size is 3842/4090 ROM and 115/128 RAM.
    | Focused scheduler, asymmetric, hygiene, history-format, allowlist, and shared-renderer
    | tests pass; the compile phase is 500/500, while the full E2E run was interrupted
    | after 78 passing cases and is not a completed full-suite certification.
    | The greedy allocator is still not globally optimal (7188/20000 exact-prefix
    | layouts have avoidable omissions; worst optimal gap 3), and same-line horizontal
    | reuse is still required for intrinsically three-way exact-Y contention.

    | The enhanced-multisprite side quest now has an experimental 192-line renderer at
    | `libraries/vcs/renderers/enhanced_multisprite/` plus public interactive example
    | `examples/18_enhanced_multisprite/01_192/01_interactive`. All six logical sprites
    | may use either TIA player: two-way vertical overlap is solid, while three-or-more
    | contention uses persistent rotating priority for fair 2-of-N service. P0 and P1
    | have intentionally asymmetric physical setup lead times but symmetric logical
    | assignment. The VBLANK scheduler sorts one-byte event indices and uses a sentinel
    | record so sprite crossings do not overrun VBLANK or add a variable visible-raster
    | path. Horizontal reuse now uses a renderer-local 160-byte packed RESP/HMP table
    | calibrated by exercising all 176 safe controls in Stella 7.0; setup line B begins
    | directly with HMOVE, and the table covers public X=0..159 without the false
    | phase assumptions from the maintained multisprite table. `vcs_enhanced_multisprite.pl`
    | certifies 997-frame Y and combined X/Y sweeps at stable 262-line NTSC timing,
    | fair three-way 2-of-3 rotation, the table hash, and both immediate-HMOVE paths.
    | Optional `vcs_enhanced_multisprite_stella.pl` / `make stella-enhanced-multisprite-test`
    | grades real Stella pixels at both X edges, swaps logical sprites across P0/P1,
    | requires solid equal-Y alignment, and checks top reach. Public example accounting
    | is currently 2498 ROM bytes / 104 RAM bytes. Next gate is user interactive Stella
    | validation, especially moving 3-6-way piles and crossing sprites. The existing
    | `renderers/multisprite/` remains unchanged and its Stella oracle still passes.

    | Disassembler corpus/hardening work through 2026-08-21 is preserved in

    | Recent completed work includes the UA/UASW duplicated-4K closeout, F8-vs-UA
    | precedence repair, Stella 4K CV storage forms, historical 4KSC tail metadata,
    | >64 KiB physical HEX/disassembly support, and conservative 4IN1/8IN1/32IN1

    | 2026-08-22 staggered 0/1/2 phosphor diagnosis: the visible flicker in the latest
    | screenshot is avoidable and is not a fairness failure.  Screen geometry resolves
    | to about X={62,72,62}, Y={86,82,76} for 0/1/2; their eight-row spans are
    | 86..79, 82..75, and 76..69, so maximum simultaneous bitmap occupancy is only
    | two.  Sprites 0 and 2 are vertically disjoint and horizontally aligned, hence
    | 0+2 can share one hardware player while 1 occupies the other.  The current
    | allocator nevertheless omits one because it colors the longer setup/event
    | reservations (P0 15 bands, P1 12, plus directional 1..6 event hazard), not the
    | visible intervals.  The all-omission queue then fairly rotates this *avoidable*
    | omission 2/2/2 over three frames.  A deterministic staggered-chain witness is
    | now in `test/vcs_enhanced_multisprite_asymmetric_scheduler.c`.  Next work should
    | be a short same-lane continuation/reuse path using the two idle bands between
    | 0's last row and 2's first row, starting with retained-X reuse, before tackling
    | true same-scanline >2 horizontal multiplexing.  See `.../enhanced_asymmetric.txt`.
    | 
    | 2026-08-22 12:04 enhanced-asymmetric follow-up: the phosphor-visible vertical
    | "jitter" is real and had a lane-dependent top-preload off-by-one.  A direct
    | real-6502 GRP trace at fixed Y showed P0 and P1 aligned for Y=85..88 and
    | 92..95, but P0 began two physical scanlines early at Y=89..91.  The P0-only
    | top-preload predecessor map now gives those three Y values one additional
    | blank state (using formerly unreachable graphics states 67..72); fixed-Y
    | P0/P1 traces are aligned exactly for Y=85..95.  The focused regression now
    | contains `--require-stable-first-visible-line` and a three-way Y=90 priority
    | rotation that fails on the old table (two-line hop) and passes on the new one.
    | 
    | The same 12:04 screenshot resolves to integer joystick geometry approximately
    | X={34,34,46,46,34,46}, Y={90,76,82,68,61,58}.  Maximum visible bitmap
    | occupancy is only two, so the user's suspicion about unnecessary flicker is
    | substantive.  Under the *current setup-reservation graph*, however, identity
    | priority's greedy allocator accepts only four; the exact 3^6 host oracle can
    | accept five, and no current-graph assignment accepts all six.  Thus this scene
    | exposes both remaining issues at once: one sprite of greedy avoidable flicker
    | (4 vs 5) and one omission caused by the renderer's long normal position/setup
    | reservations even though the bitmap intervals are two-colorable.  The host
    | scheduler test now pins that 4/5/6 distinction.  Do not "fix" this by merely
    | lowering P0/P1 same-lane gap constants: P1 gap-10 relaxation can schedule a
    | sprite that never reaches GRP, while a P0 gap-14 experiment produced a 264-raw-
    | line frame in a deterministic scene.  The next raster change must be a genuine
    | retained-X/short-continuation event, with the exact host oracle used separately
    | to develop bounded lane rebalancing.
    | 
    | 2026-08-22 retained-X test-repair checkpoint: the first packaged retained-X
    | experiment exposed two structural test failures and one hidden VBLANK timing
    | regression.  `vcs_renderer_branch_contracts.pl` was correct to reject the two
    | new `.flex` branches.  The redundant far below-P0 branch was removed, and the
    | long AssignRank loop is now written explicitly as a local `beq.same` exit plus
    | `jmp`, preserving the assembler long-branch cycle shape while restoring an
    | explicit timing contract.  The asymmetric structural hazard test was stale:
    | ordinary P1-above-P0 gaps 1..6 remain unsafe, but retained P0 deliberately makes
    | exact gap 6 safe by moving the P0 action one band later.  The test now pins the
    | 1..5 rejection plus the narrowly qualified retained-P0 +6 exception in both
    | directions instead of requiring the pre-retained blanket shape.
    | 
    | The retained record builder was also too expensive on every ordinary accepted
    | sprite and could extend randomized stress frames to 265 raw harness lines.
    | ChooseLane now returns ordinary lanes as 0/1, retained lanes as $80/$81, and
    | omission as 2.  The hot assignment loop folds retained dispatch into the
    | accept/reject test, keeps the common non-retained record path close to the old
    | fast path, and uses a separate retained record builder that skips coarse RESP
    | slot calculation entirely.  The three 5000-frame combined X/Y stress seeds are
    | back at stable 264 raw harness lines.  Public size remains 3980/4090 ROM and
    | 115/128 RAM.  The two originally reported tests pass; the complete compile
    | phase is 500/500.  A full E2E run was started and reached 78/319 passing before
    | the execution timeout killed the make process, so do not claim full-suite
    | completion from this checkpoint.
    | 
    | 2026-08-22 16:41 bridge-orientation closeout: the disappearing `3` and malformed
    | `4` top were traced to the retained-X same-column bridge being colored according
    | to traversal priority.  The current scheduler detects equal-X endpoints 12 bands
    | apart plus a middle sprite at the 7/5 split and forces the only safe orientation
    | (endpoints P1, bridge P0).  The exact fixture uses X={0,0,62,72,62,140},
    | Y={95,95,71,64,59,12}.  Identity priority and inherited priority
    | {0,1,2,4,3,5} are permanent real-GRP regressions; the development sweep covered
    | all 720 priority permutations and rendered mask $3f.  The extra VBLANK work moved
    | the current extended deadline split from the interim 57/19 to 63/13.  Preserve
    | this implementation; the next action is user full-suite/Stella validation, not
    | rediscovery of the bridge geometry.  The user then ran the full E2E suite and got
    | 318/319: only `vcs_examples_build_2of8.test` failed because the recursive example
    | smoke builder compiled this source without the two Makefile-required defines
    | `VCS_NTSC_EXTENDED_VBLANK` and `MULTISPRITE_NO_RETAINED_PF_ROWS`, overflowing 4K.
    | `vcs_examples_build.pl` now mirrors those public-example flags for

    | was a smoke-harness fidelity bug, not a renderer-size regression.

    | VCSC MAIN ROADMAP
    | =================
    | 
    | Purpose
    | -------
    | This file preserves the detailed project roadmap formerly embedded in
    | `.../context.txt`. The compact context file states the active workstream and
    | next action; this file owns the detailed main-roadmap checklist and acceptance
    | criteria. `.../bankswitching.txt` separately owns the detailed bankswitching
    | sub-roadmap.
    | 
    | Do not duplicate completed implementation history here. Record completed work
    | in the appropriate daily file under `.../context-history/`, then update the
    | checkboxes and current-state wording here.
    | 
    | Immediate TODO
    | --------------
    | The first unfinished leaf item is the default next action. Parent items remain
    | unchecked until every child is complete.
    | 
    | Current next action: Item 43, add the public console/TIA diagnostic cartridge.
    | Main Item 42 and focused bankswitching Item 32 (OMNI) are complete; CV/CommaVid is bankswitching Item 33.
    | 
    | [x] 0. Repair the shared 181-line renderer by deriving its beam schedule
    |        directly from the proven 192-line renderer.
    | 
    |        Completed 2026-07-28:
    | 
    |        * Replace the compact inherited raster body with the proven four-write
    |          schedule. The 181-line variant differs only in its eleven-row limit,
    |          score-component handoff, and terminal accounting.
    |        * Preserve exact playfield write phases at CPU cycles 10, 17, 40, and 47
    |          for PF1-left, PF2-left, PF2-right, and PF1-right respectively.
    |        * Compensate the score handoff for P0 without changing the steady raster,
    |          retain exact 262-line frames, and keep example 13 within 4K ROM.
    |        * Port the same visible behavior to the unofficial-enabled twin while
    |          retaining its two reviewed AXS substitutions and one zero-page NOP.
    |        * Certify examples 07 and 08 against fresh Stella output using their
    |          literal C26 playfield rows: 11 rows x 16 scanlines x 32 logical bits,
    |          5,632 positions per example, zero mismatches.
    |        * Strengthen the permanent raster checks to validate all eleven rows and
    |          all four beam-critical writes instead of accepting the former broken
    |          register choreography.
    |        * Remove the obsolete standalone candidate patch now that its work is
    |          integrated.
    | 
    | 
    | [x] 1. Initially return retained scalar/pointer values in A:X. Task 18a
    |        now supersedes this with callee-owned memory returns.
    | 
    | [x] 2. Make direct fixed parameters callee-owned symbols and reject call cycles.
    | 
    | [x] 3. Enforce legal return types and delete the caller-owned return-slot ABI.
    | 
    | [x] 4. Fix libraries/vcs/vcs_4k.cfg so mirrored RIOT RAM is not modeled as two
    |        independent memories. The initial fixed reserve was later replaced by
    |        call-graph-derived hardware-stack sizing.
    | 
    | [x] 5. Replace stack-backed automatic locals with fixed-address per-function
    |        storage. Start with distinct frames; defer frame overlay optimization.
    | 
    | [x] 6. Inventory every retained _pushN/_popN use and classify it as automatic
    |        local allocation, expression temporary, caller argument scratch,
    |        aggregate-only support, or obsolete runtime support.
    | 
    | [x] 7. Replace ordinary direct-call argument/result scratch with fixed call-site
    |        storage. Leave the indirect-call path isolated on the old stack
    |        temporarily.
    | 
    | [x] 8. Remove variadic functions, stdarg machinery, and the now-isolated legacy
    |        direct-call software-stack fallback.
    | 
    | [x] 9. Remove function pointers and the indirect-call software-stack ABI.
    | 
    | [x] 10. Replace retained expression, statement, lvalue, and initializer
    |         temporary storage for the 8/16-bit language with fixed BSS scratch.
    |         Slice 15e later pooled that scratch by function and nesting depth.
    | 
    | [x] 11. Remove operator overloading, `$exactops`, and weak built-in operator
    |         dispatch. Compiler source now emits zero `_pushN`/`_popN` calls. Keep
    |         pooled scratch temporarily used `_vcsc_fp`; task 18b later removed that
    |         final runtime indirection after `_vcsc_sp` and the stack helpers were gone.
    | 
    | [x] 12. Enforce the final basic-type set: int8_t, uint8_t, int16_t, uint16_t
    |         in controlled groups.
    | 
    |         [x] 12a. Remove floating-point types, literals, compiler/runtime code,
    |                   driver linkage, and positive tests.
    |         [x] 12b. Remove big-endian and mixed-endian type/expression support.
    |         [x] 12c. Remove 24/32/64-bit integer types, helpers, and positive
    |                   tests; enforce one- and two-byte target integers only.
    |         [x] 12d. Replace transitional compiler-required `bool`, `char`, and
    |                   `int` names with uint8_t, int8_t, and int16_t so the stock
    |                   machine interface exposes only the canonical four integer
    |                   names.
    | 
    | [x] 13. Produce and verify an unbanked 4K VCS cartridge using the static-frame
    |         ABI and the real 128-byte shared RAM model. The example emits a raw
    |         4096-byte .bin and has an automated map/vector/size regression test.
    | 
    | [x] 14. Resolve source-level absolute `ref` names in inline assembly using
    |         opcode-aware access semantics. Reads require a read address, writes
    |         require a write address, and read-modify-write instructions require
    |         identical non-`none` read/write addresses. Reject all mismatches rather
    |         than warning or falling back.
    | 
    | [x] Side quest: add a second complete 4K example that plays a repeating
    |     frame-driven classical melody from a const ROM array of volume, frequency,
    |     control, and timing structs, plus reusable NTSC TIA sound aliases. The
    |     corrected player runs inside a fixed TIM64T overscan budget, uses two final
    |     WSYNCs, and has an execution regression proving Stella-reported 262-line
    |     frames rather than the former 261-line output.
    | 
    | [x] 15. Finish the general compiler-efficiency work exposed by Ode to Joy
    |         before continuing broad functional reduction. The source-level
    |         direct-index player, compact byte paths, lifetime-pooled scratch, and
    |         repeated-address reuse now pass the RAM and fixed-overscan gates.
    | 
    |         Acceptance criteria:
    | 
    |         [x] 15a. Express `music_tick()` in `ode_to_joy.c26`; retain the const ROM
    |                   array of `MusicStep` structs and remove the companion player
    |                   assembly once the source version passes.
    |         [x] 15b. Fit the complete cartridge in the VCS RAM map without relying
    |                   on accidental spare bytes or moving ordinary game logic into
    |                   assembly. Whole-program call-graph sizing replaced the fixed
    |                   32-byte hardware-stack partition.
    |         [x] 15c. Keep every execution path--ordinary counter tick, note change,
    |                   silent gap, and end-of-score wrap--inside the fixed 30-line
    |                   TIM64T overscan budget while preserving stable Stella-reported
    |                   262-line NTSC frames in the execution regression.
    |         [x] 15d. Make natural direct `music[index].field` practical: inline-scale
    |                   a simple uint8_t index for power-of-two element sizes without
    |                   an indextmp or multiply helper; emit compact ordinary uint8_t
    |                   increment/comparison/constant-store paths; restore the Ode
    |                   source to direct indexing; retain zero BSS scratch and stable
    |                   262-line timing. A high-byte regression covers index 64 into
    |                   four-byte elements.

    |                   Unsupported index forms use generic pooled scratch.

    |                   [x] 16c1. Correct pointer-pointer subtraction typing and
    |                             lowering. Compatible pointers now produce `int16_t`

    |                             elements use sign-preserving division by object size.
    |                             Incompatible pointed-to types are rejected. Tests
    |                             cover positive and negative byte and three-byte
    |                             element differences plus result width.
    |                   [x] 16c2. Preserve pointer declarators and array-to-pointer

    |                             path cannot intercept pointer-targeted decay.
    |                             Fixed scratch retains the destination declarator.
    |                             Tests cover local, global, file-static, function-
    |                             static, and indirect pointer destinations with one-
    |                             and two-byte element arrays.

    | [x] 17. Rename the project and toolchain to `vcsc` as a hard cut. The public
    |         driver is `vcsc`; companion tools are `vcsc-cc1`, `vcsc-as`, `vcsc-ld`,
    |         `vcsc-ar`, and `vcsc-sim`. Source files use `.c26`; the runtime lives in
    |         `libraries/runtime` and installs `libvcsc.l26` plus `vcsc-runtime.inc`.
    |         Generated/runtime symbols use VCSC prefixes. Relocatable objects use
    |         `.o26` with the VCSC-specific `\x01\x00o26` magic; archives use
    |         `.l26` with `VCSL26\x01`. No compatibility wrappers or readers preserve
    |         obsolete artifact names or magics.
    |         Every maintained documentation file starts with the canonical lowercase
    |         `vcsc` Standard-font FIGlet banner. Historical changelog entries retain
    |         old spellings where needed to describe what actually happened.

    |                   `frame_ntsc.c26` should publish the standard NTSC phase sizes

    |                   `vcs_ntsc_vsync()`, `vcs_ntsc_begin_vblank()`,

    |                   `vcs_ntsc_end_overscan()`, and a scanline-wait helper. The
    |                   application `main()` remains the composition scheduler and
    |                   chooses component order, blank lines, and which callbacks run.

    |                   private implementation details rather than becoming an API
    |                   that lets callers omit the required transition sequence.

    |                   The scheduler must assert VBLANK and start the shared VBLANK
    |                   deadline before invoking any `TEMPLATE_vblank()` function;
    |                   callbacks share the remaining budget and must not own VBLANK,
    |                   WSYNC, TIM1T/TIM8T/TIM64T/T1024T, INTIM, or TIMINT. The public
    |                   end-VBLANK operation must distinguish expiry from remaining
    |                   time, wait only for the unused remainder, issue the final WSYNC,
    |                   and clear VBLANK at the beginning of the visible scanline. Apply
    |                   the same scheduler-owned deadline model to overscan. A production
    |                   overrun continues with one long frame; optional diagnostics set
    |                   sticky phase bits rather than returning mandatory status.

    |           with required `parameter lines;` and calibrated `lines:=192` / `lines:=181`
    |           profiles. The component owns 79 retained state bytes plus two public P0

    |           P0 remains legal over X=0..159 when a score owns P0 immediately before or
    |           after gameplay. Restore the faithful three-cycle zero-page mask reads,
    |           keep K1 handlers close enough for real short branches, constrain every
    |           timing-sensitive branch to same-page geometry, keep HMCLR on its three-cycle

    |           cycle-critical `(ptr),Y` glyph fetch from page crossing, including P0 at its

    |           could run beyond the scanline near the right edge. Public P1 X is never
    |           mutated. The 181 score/P0 handoff now delays raster entry by three CPU cycles
    |           so its first/topmost rank uses the same packed-X phase as every later rank;
    |           do not resurrect the old wrapped +9 lookup, which aliased X=143..151 into the
    |           ordinary right-edge bucket and caused a visible jump/wrap. For 181 P0, precompute

    |           Stella proves this keeps P0 fixed when vertical sorting changes.

    |           reused the faithful persistent sort table as packed horizontal control
    |           storage, then restored identity order every VBLANK. Overlapping logical P1
    |           sprites therefore acquired permanent sprite-number priority instead of
    |           flickering. Preserve the exact five-value persistent order across frames;
    |           it is now nibble-packed into three retained bytes before state[60..64] is
    |           reused for packed horizontal controls. Six-frame MOS6502/TIA overlap
    |           regressions require logical P1/P2 to alternate as the visible winner in 192

    |           phase repair aligns the first reposition with the ordinary post-WSYNC phase

    | [x] 31. Add fixed-point whole-program specialization and inlining for
    |         reachable internal functions with exactly one direct call site. Inline
    |         through the compiler IR/control-flow representation, not textual
    |         substitution; fold arguments/returns and simplify newly exposed code, then
    |         recompute the call graph, lifetime analysis, activation/scratch overlay,
    |         peak RIOT RAM usage, linked ROM size, and hardware-stack depth. Because VCSC
    |         has no recursion, inlining must not by itself destroy RAM-overlay
    |         opportunities: activation areas that are mutually exclusive in the call
    |         graph remain mutually exclusive after inlining. Treat an unexpected peak-RAM
    |         increase from equivalent lifetimes as an allocator/lifetime-analysis defect,
    |         not as an inherent inlining tradeoff.
    | 
    |         Make ROM placement and timing constraints part of the inlining proof. An
    |         inline candidate is legal only when final placement can still satisfy every
    |         `.same`/`.cross` branch annotation and applicable `align()` constraint;
    |         retain the separate function when inlining makes those constraints
    |         unsatisfiable. Exclude exported or address-taken functions, assembly ABI
    |         entry points, weak/required hooks, and other boundaries whose ABI or timing
    |         contract requires a distinct function. After legal placement, use measured
    |         linked ROM size and hardware-stack depth to decide whether inlining is a net
    |         win, and retain the separate function when measured linked code grows.
    | 
    |         Before or independently of inlining, specialize parameters at single call
    |         sites so RAM used only to communicate already-known values or addresses can
    |         disappear. For a by-value parameter that is explicitly `const`, or is
    |         implicitly const because the callee never writes it and never passes it to
    |         code that may write it, avoid a separate callee RAM slot when the caller's
    |         actual storage is proven stable for the formal's live range; use the
    |         caller's storage directly. For every single-callsite `ref` parameter --
    |         regular, `const`, or `writeonly` -- bake the known actual address into the
    |         specialized callee and eliminate RAM used to pass/store that address. Run
    |         constant propagation, dead-code elimination, addressing-mode relaxation,
    |         and lifetime/overlay analysis again after specialization so the gains can
    |         cascade even when the function itself cannot be inlined.
    | 
    |         Cover multi-return control flow, nested one-call chains, exact ROM-size
    |         wins/losses, stack-depth reduction, `.same`/`.cross` placement failures,
    |         alignment failures, preservation of pre-inline RAM-overlay opportunities,
    |         explicit and implicit const-value specialization, stability failures that
    |         require retaining a by-value copy, and address specialization for regular,
    |         `const`, and `writeonly` refs. Verify exact linked ROM, peak RIOT RAM, and
    |         hardware-stack depth for representative acceptance and rejection cases.
    |

    |         `.../inline_roadmap.txt`. Automatic single-callsite `ref` and readonly
    |         by-value specialization removes redundant parameter/address RAM where
    |         proven safe. Measured whole-program function inlining is available via
    |         opt-in `-finline-profit`; it trial-links legal candidates to a fixed point

    |           simulator/disassembler/install/package regression coverage.
    | 
    | [x] 42. Generalize automatic code/RODATA placement across compatible regions in
    |         every multi-region cartridge topology.
    | 
    |         Completed 2026-08-17:
    | 
    |         * Separated "multiple placement regions" from "requires mapper switching".
    |           Selector-controlled banks and selector-free direct islands now share the
    |           same deterministic whole-layout CODE/RODATA allocator. Direct topologies
    |           populate logical placement records even though `cartridge_banked` remains
    |           false.
    |         * Preserved explicit source placement as a hard pin. `main` and every
    |           `_`-prefixed startup/runtime function are hard-pinned to the unique
    |           startup/home region; explicit contradictory placement is now diagnosed.
    |         * Permitted one selector-free direct region to carry `$startup`. For direct
    |           mappings this is only a placement/home marker and creates no selector
    |           access, wrong-bank startup recovery, vector bridge, or trampoline.
    |         * Kept switched-mapper semantics intact: ordinary nonreplicated ROM data
    |           references and retained branches remain same-bank constraints; direct
    |           JSR/JMP cuts remain weighted soft preferences implemented by trampolines;
    |           replica locality and weighted hardware-return depth remain authoritative.
    |         * For selector-free direct mappings, absolute JSR/JMP/data references across
    |           regions are ordinary 16-bit references and create neither same-region
    |           unions nor trampoline/cut costs.
    |         * Added an exact ROM-placement dry run before accepting each candidate. It
    |           mirrors final source-order allocation, branch/page low-byte constraints,
    |           alignment, holes, initialized-DATA load images, and generated copy/zero/init
    |           tables. This closes the latent raw-byte-budget bug exposed by Tanks, where a
    |           nominally fitting near-full startup bank could overflow during final layout.
    |         * Deliberately did NOT generalize writable-memory placement. Ordinary
    |           unqualified DATA/BSS/ZEROPAGE keep the configured default RAM (RIOT zero
    |           page in the VCS profiles); named RAM such as `cartram` remains an explicit
    |           programmer decision because generated/assembly paths may depend on zero-page
    |           addressing and timing.
    |         * Audited split RIOT/Superchip initialization. Startup already emits one
    |           object-specific copy/zero record per allocated DATA/BSS object; Superchip
    |           records carry separate readable `$F080-$F0FF` and writable `$F000-$F07F`
    |           aliases, while ordinary zero-page records keep their RIOT addresses. No
    |           contiguous-RAM assumption or RAM-placement change was needed.
    |         * Extended `vcs_direct_8k.c26` with a direct `$startup` home region and a
    |           regression that overfills that home region with unqualified whole functions,
    |           proving automatic spill into the second direct island while `main` remains
    |           home and no hotspots, bridges, or trampolines are generated.
    |         * Removed every explicit `bank0`/`bank1` code/RODATA qualifier from
    |           `examples/01_basic/13_tanks/`. The example retains its explicit `cartram`
    |           RAM qualifiers. Its F8SC map now proves the linker automatically partitions
    |           ordinary functions and constants across both banks, including placing the
    |           large overscan function in bank1 while keeping `main` and startup code in
    |           bank0. The existing emulator-backed Tanks oracle remains green.
    |         * Generalized `BANK PLACEMENT` maps and `--explain-bank-placement` reporting
    |           to direct multi-region profiles. The option name remains as a compatibility
    |           spelling; direct maps report placement but still have no `TRAMPOLINES`
    |           section.
    | 
    | [ ] 43. Add a public diagnostic cartridge that exercises and reports the supported
    |         console switches, controller types, TIA objects, playfield, colors, collisions,
    |         and audio. Keep the displayed results unambiguous, include deterministic
    |         self-test screens where practical, document required controller hookups, and

    |
